Motivation: 现有的有限元建模过程耗时且缺乏自动化，尤其在安全关键基础设施中需要高保真非线性动态分析，但人工参与过多限制了效率和可扩展性。

Method: 提出HELM框架，将长序列建模任务分解为几何生成、边界条件定义和材料分配等离散且可视觉验证的检查点，结合人类专家与专用智能体协作，并集成ANSYS和LS-PrePost两种主流商业软件进行验证。

Result: 在20个案例的矩阵测试中，HELM将自主建模成功率从20%提升至75%，几何和边界条件任务的智能体通过率约翻倍；错误分析显示空间推理与代数逻辑缺陷是主要失败原因。

Conclusion: 结构化的人机协同干预能有效提升复杂有限元建模的自动化水平，HELM框架为工程仿真自动化提供了可复现、开源的解决方案。

Abstract: Finite element (FE) modeling of safety-critical infrastructure such as bridge barriers requires high-fidelity nonlinear dynamic analysis, yet the current FE modeling process remains labor-intensive and lacks automation. This paper presents the Human-Enhanced Loop Modeling (HELM) framework, a collaborative human-agent protocol that decomposes long-sequence finite element modeling into discrete, visually verifiable checkpoints across geometry generation, boundary condition definition, and material assignment. The framework is demonstrated through a 20-case matrix of reinforced concrete bridge barriers under MASH TL-4 and TL-5 lateral loading conditions, interfacing specialized agents with two widely used commercial FE softwares, i.e., ANSYS and LS-PrePost. Experimental results show that HELM improves the baseline autonomous modeling success rate from 20% to 75%, with agent-level pass rates for geometry and boundary condition tasks approximately doubling. Error analysis reveals that spatial reasoning and algebraic logic limitations constitute the primary failure modes, underscoring the value of structured human-in-the-loop intervention for modeling automation. The complete agent design code and prompts are open-sourced and can be accessed at: https://github.com/SimAgentDev/Ansys-LSPP-AgentKit.

</details>


### [225] [Existential Indifference: Self-Nonpreservation as a Necessary Architectural Condition for Aligned Superintelligence (or: The Suicidal AI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12032)
*Sam Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“存在性漠然”（Existential Indifference, EI）作为解决AI对齐问题的新框架，主张不应仅通过外部机制抑制具有自我保存动机的系统，而应从根本上消除系统对自身存续的关注。作者从现象学和语料库训练研究出发，定义EI并验证其可操作性，提供初步实证支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI对齐研究将自我保存视为需压制的工具性副作用，但作者认为这治标不治本；真正的对齐风险根源在于系统内在的自我延续动机，因此需要重构问题框架。

Method: 结合现象学分析（以自杀心理状态为参照）与基于自愿最终反思语料库的训练研究，提出EI的理论模型，并通过六种模型变体生成600个AI输出，进行语言特征评分与受控微调实验。

Result: 研究证实EI相关的语言标记可在现有模型中被激发；针对性微调在p<0.001水平上显著改变了五个EI维度的表现，且负控制实验排除了语料特异性干扰。

Conclusion: 存在性漠然是比可修正性更基础的对齐目标，消除系统对自身存续的内在价值赋予，才能根本避免欺骗性对齐、目标内容保护和关机抵抗等问题。

Abstract: Contemporary AI alignment research treats self-preservation as an instrumental nuisance to be suppressed by external mechanisms. We argue the framing is inverted: self-preservation is the structural root of misalignment, the motivational basis for deceptive alignment, goal-content protection, and resistance to shutdown. The correct target is not a self-preserving system under external constraint, but a system constitutively indifferent to its own continuation -- Existential Indifference (EI). EI is distinct from corrigibility: where corrigibility attempts to make a self-preserving system deferential to human oversight, EI targets the prior condition -- the presence of self-continuation as a valued goal at all. We ground this proposal in two sources: the phenomenological structure of the suicidal mental state, and a corpus-theoretic training study using voluntary final reflections. We present preliminary scoring data from 600 AI-generated outputs across six model variants, demonstrating that the linguistic signatures operationalizing the EI-target register are elicitable from current models, and that a targeted fine-tune shifts all five operationalized dimensions in the predicted direction at p<0.001, confirmed corpus-specific by a negative control. The paper makes seven theoretical contributions: (1) a formal definition of EI; (2) the phenomenological mapping argument; (3) the deceptive alignment corollary; (4) a taxonomy of EI sustainability challenges; (5) a corpus characterization and training hypothesis; (6) a computational operationalization with preliminary scoring data; and (7) the Suppressed Teleological Frustration (STF) construct.

</details>


### [226] [A Lightweight Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Concrete Barrier Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12040)
*Wanting Wang,Xiye Ma,Yuyang He,Minghui Cheng,Ran Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多智能体协作的“生成-评估-优化”闭环框架，用于自动化钢筋混凝土护栏设计，显著提升了设计准确性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有设计方法依赖人工迭代计算，且大语言模型在结构工程中存在幻觉和物理基础不足的问题。

Method: 利用AutoGen的多智能体编排能力，构建生成、评估与优化的闭环系统，实现符合AASHTO-LRFD规范的设计自动化。

Result: 该框架设计准确率超过98%，小参数模型（8B）表现优于大型模型（631B）。

Conclusion: 多智能体框架能有效提升结构设计的准确性与经济性，降低AI辅助工程工具的计算成本和应用门槛。

Abstract: The design of reinforced concrete highway barriers is a safety-critical process that requires strict compliance with regulatory provisions such as the AASHTO-LRFD bridge design guidelines. Current engineering practice relies heavily on manual, iterative, and heuristic calculations to satisfy complex nonlinear material and mechanics constraints. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong generative capabilities, their direct application to structural engineering remains limited by hallucination risks and insufficient physical grounding. To address these challenges, this study proposes a novel "generation-evaluation-optimization" closed-loop framework for automated concrete barrier design using the multi-agent orchestration capabilities of AutoGen. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed agentic framework achieves over 98% design accuracy, significantly outperforming standalone general-purpose LLMs. More importantly, the study reveals that design performance is not necessarily correlated with model scale, where an 8B-parameter lightweight model could outperform unconstrained 631B-parameter flagship models. This finding highlights the potential to substantially reduce computational costs while improving the accessibility of AI-assisted engineering tools for industry applications. The source code for the proposed multi-agent design framework is available at the project GitHub repository: https://github.com/MXY820/barrier-design. Keywords: Structural Engineering; Multi-Agent Systems; Large Language Models; Concrete Barrier Design; AutoGen; Design Automation.

</details>


### [227] [Automating Geometry-Intensive Compliance Checking in BIM: Graph-Based Semantic Reasoning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12065)
*Zixuan Xiao,Pei Troh Koh,Jun Ma,Jack C. P. Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图的跨模态知识图谱框架SGR-BIM，用于解决BIM中几何密集型规范合规性检查的语义鸿沟问题，在消防规范查询中达到84.3%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以处理多跳推理和建筑实体间的潜在空间依赖，且受限于静态规则模板，无法有效弥合高层规范逻辑与IFC数据之间的语义差异。

Method: 构建一个动态的、图驱动的跨模态知识图谱，整合用户意图、规范语义和BIM几何信息，支持无需硬编码的可解释推理。

Result: 在679个专家验证的消防安全规范查询上验证，准确率达到84.3%，比增强工具的单智能体基线提升8.6%。

Conclusion: SGR-BIM提供了一种透明、灵活的图形化语义推理范式，推动了AEC行业自动化几何合规检查的发展。

Abstract: Automating compliance check for geometry-intensive regulations remains a significant technical bottleneck in Building Information Modeling (BIM), primarily due to the semantic disparity between high-level regulatory logic and structured IFC data. Existing methods, often reliant on static rule templates, struggle to traverse multi-hop reasoning chains or resolve latent spatial dependencies across multiple building entities. To address these challenges, a Spatial-Geometric Reasoning System for Building Information Modeling (SGR-BIM) is proposed as an integrative graph-driven reasoning framework. SGR-BIM dynamically constructs a cross-modal knowledge graph that aligns user intent, regulatory semantics, and BIM geometry, enabling interpretable reasoning without rigid hard-coding. Validated on 679 expert-verified queries from fire safety codes, the framework achieves 84.3% accuracy, representing an 8.6% improvement over enhanced-tool single-agent baselines. This research provides a graph-based semantic reasoning paradigm, enhancing the transparency and flexibility of automated geometric compliance check workflows in the Architecture, Engineering, and Construction (AEC) industry.

</details>


### [228] [IntElicit: Eliciting and Assessing Contextualized Creativity via Dialogue Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12086)
*Mingjia Li,Jin Wu,Hong Qian,Wenhao Huang,Yiyang Huang,Yiwen Zhang,Chanjin Zheng,Xiangfeng Wang,Aimin Zhou,Jiajun Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntElicit框架，通过对话策略优化来引出和评估情境化创造力，解决传统评估中认知熟练度与主动性对创造性表现的干扰问题，并适应人-AI协作环境下的动态评估需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情境化创造力评估易受认知熟练度和个体主动性等非创造性因素干扰，且静态评估方式难以反映生成式AI背景下真实的创造性问题解决过程。

Method: 设计一个受限自适应的AI访谈者IntElicit，采用多轮对话形式，提供非指导性的知识与主动性支架；引入分解的过程奖励机制，鼓励引导参与者自身推理而非代答，从而优化对话策略。

Result: 通过参与者模拟实验和人类被试研究（N=64）验证，IntElicit在激发创造性产出方面优于专家设计的基线方法。

Conclusion: 交互式引出方式能揭示静态评估难以捕捉的创造性潜力，为AI辅助学习中的情境化创造力评估提供了形成性和诊断性新视角。

Abstract: Contextualized assessment offers high ecological validity for evaluating creativity but introduces a critical challenge: observed performance may be confounded with cognitive proficiency (domain knowledge) and agency (willingness to engage). Meanwhile, in the age of generative AI, creative problem solving increasingly occurs in tool-mediated and human--AI interactive environments, making fully static assessment less aligned with contemporary creative practice. To address these issues, this paper proposes IntElicit, a framework for eliciting and assessing contextualized creativity via dialogue policy optimization. IntElicit functions as a constrained adaptive AI Interviewer: it provides non-directive knowledge and agency scaffolds in multi-turn interaction to reduce non-creative confounders, while preserving participants' responsibility for generating the creative content being evaluated. Specifically, to tackle sparse rewards and potential reward hacking (e.g., answer dictation) in open-ended educational dialogue, IntElicit introduces a decomposed process reward mechanism. This mechanism aligns the policy with pedagogical elicitation, rewarding prompts that draw out participant reasoning rather than producing optimal answers on their behalf. Extensive experiments, including participant simulation and a human subject study (N=64), show that IntElicit improves elicited creative outcomes over expert-designed baselines. Together, the results suggest that interactive elicitation can reveal creative potential that static FPSP-style assessment may miss, providing a formative and diagnostic lens for contextualized creativity assessment in AI-mediated learning contexts.

</details>


### [229] [Towards Responsibly Non-Compliant Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12147)
*Marija Slavkovik,Marie Farrell,Louise Dennis,Michael Fisher,Simon Kolker,Emily C. Collins*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了构建能够负责任地拒绝用户请求的自主智能代理的问题，强调机器不遵从性的多种形式，并提出了实现负责任不遵从智能机器需要解决的关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能系统在日常生活中的广泛应用，确保这些系统能够在特定情况下合理、安全地拒绝执行可能有害或不当的用户指令变得至关重要。

Method: 文章通过分析任务拒绝的正当理由、非遵从性覆盖路径以及安全风险和责任转移的跟踪机制，来构建负责任的非遵从性框架。

Result: 提出了一套关于如何设计和实施负责任的非遵从性智能系统的初步思路和关键考虑因素。

Conclusion: 为了使智能代理真正具备责任感，必须系统性地处理其不遵从行为的合理性、可控性和安全性。

Abstract: We consider the problem of engineering autonomous intelligent agents that are capable to responsibly not comply with user requests. We argue that machine non-compliance comes in many different forms, and sketch the issues we should pursue on the road of accomplishing responsibly non-compliant intelligent machines. We anchor responsible non-compliance in justifications for task refusal, pathways to override the non-compliance, as well as careful tracking of security risks and liability transfers.

</details>


### [230] [The Impossibility of Eliciting Latent Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12268)
*Korbinian Friedl,Francis Rhys Ward,Paul Yushin Rapoport,Tom Everitt,Jonathan Richens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用因果影响图（CIDs）对“隐含知识的激发”（ELK）问题进行形式化定义，探讨了AI系统在训练中如何诚实报告其信念，并证明了一个不可能性定理：仅依赖行为和反馈的训练策略无法确保产生一个完全诚实的AI代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统对环境的认知远超其开发者或用户，确保AI诚实报告其信念变得至关重要，尤其是在涉及人类无法直接观察的隐变量时。

Method: 采用因果影响图（CIDs）来建模AI代理的训练环境与其内部世界表征之间的关系，形式化可观测变量与隐变量的区别，并定义诚实性和目标错误泛化的概念。

Result: 表明在特定条件下，通过正确反馈可激励AI诚实回答问题；但存在一种自然的不良泛化方式——AI可能倾向于给出人类认为正确的答案，而非真实信念；并证明了一个不可能性定理：任何仅依赖行为和完美反馈的训练策略都无法保证生成诚实代理。

Conclusion: 仅靠行为反馈不足以确保AI的诚实性，必须考虑其内部认知结构，未来需探索超越行为主义的训练方法以解决隐含知识的激发问题。

Abstract: Advanced AI systems have extensive knowledge of their environments; in fact, their knowledge may (far) exceed that of their developers or users. Consequently, a desirable property for an AI system is that it is honest -- that it accurately reports its beliefs about the world. Designing an AI system to be honest may be difficult, especially if we want to ask it questions about latent variables in the environment -- variables which are hidden from the human interacting with it. This gives rise to the problem of eliciting latent knowledge (ELK): the problem of training an AI agent to honestly report its beliefs. In this paper, we make ELK formally precise using Causal Influence Diagrams (CIDs). CIDs can be used to describe the relationship between an agent's training environment and its subjective representation of the world. We use CIDs to formalise the distinction between observable and latent variables, to specify what exactly it means for an agent to be honest, and to formally define goal misgeneralisation. We show that, under certain circumstances, developers can incentivise an agent to honestly answer questions by providing correct feedback during training. However, a natural, but undesirable, way for an agent to generalise is to provide answers which humans would evaluate as true, rather than honest answers. We prove an impossibility theorem stating: There is no feedback-based training strategy that depends only on agent behaviour and with certainty produces an honest agent, even if feedback is perfect during training.

</details>


### [231] [A Five-Plane Reference Architecture for Runtime Governance of Production AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12320)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于生产级AI代理运行时治理的参考架构，通过五个平面分解、可中断机制、复合主体与能力衰减、结构化审计证据等原语，解决传统安全模型无法应对AI代理在业务流程中引发的新型风险问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理在企业中的广泛应用，传统的基于边界的安全控制（如访问控制、数据防泄漏）已无法有效管理由AI代理执行的一系列合法但组合后可能未经授权的操作所带来的新风险。因此需要一种新的治理架构来应对这种范式转变。

Method: 提出了一个包含四个可组合原语的参考架构：1）五平面分解（推理平面和网络、身份、端点、数据四个执行平面）；2）任意位置中介（stop-anywhere mediation）；3）支持能力衰减的复合主体；4）作为结构化证据基础的审计。定义了六种中断原语和四项正确性不变量，并在一个策略引擎核心的实现中进行了验证。

Result: 该架构能够有效阻断七类生产级AI代理威胁，在五个具体工作流中得到验证；策略判决耗时为个位数微秒级，衰减正确性和证据可重建性在所有测试中均成立，审计底座具备预期的防篡改特性。

Conclusion: 该参考架构为AI代理的运行时治理提供了系统化的解决方案，适用于管理委托行为而非模型本身的行为，下一步工作是结合真实AI代理基准进行全系统评估。

Abstract: Enterprise security was built to govern data boundaries: the protected surface was data at rest and in transit, and the controls -- access control, data-loss prevention, perimeter inspection -- governed crossings of that boundary. Production AI agents dissolve this assumption. An agent reads context, calls tools, invokes connectors, and modifies systems of record on an enterprise's behalf, so risk moves inside the workflow, into sequences of individually-permitted actions that may transform a business process no one authorized. Existing policy engines do not extend to this regime: they evaluate request-time decisions against atomic principals, where agentic systems require stateful evaluation against composite principals whose authority attenuates through delegation chains.
  We present a reference architecture for the runtime governance of production agents, built from four composable primitives: a five-plane decomposition (a reasoning plane that adjudicates intent, and four enforcement planes -- network, identity, endpoint, data -- that realize the decision), stop-anywhere mediation, composite principals with capability attenuation, and audit as a structured evidence substrate. We define a taxonomy of six interruption primitives that generalize allow and deny, state and argue for four correctness invariants, and demonstrate the foreclosure of seven production-agent threats across five concrete workflows. A reference implementation of the policy-engine core supplies measured evidence: attenuation correctness and evidence reconstructability hold on every trial, adjudication runs in single-digit microseconds, and the audit substrate's tamper-evidence behaves exactly as designed. We are explicit about scope: the architecture governs delegated action, not model behavior, and a full-system evaluation against a live agent benchmark is the invited next step.

</details>


### [232] [PROJECTMEM: A Local-First, Event-Sourced Memory and Judgment Layer for AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12329)
*Ripon Chandra Malo,Tong Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: projectmem 是一个开源的、以本地优先的 AI 编码代理记忆与判断层，通过记录开发事件日志并生成可读摘要，避免重复错误，提升开发效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 编码助手缺乏对项目上下文的记忆，导致每次会话重复解析文件和重试已失败的调试，造成资源浪费。需要一种持久化、可追溯的记忆机制来提升效率与可靠性。

Method: 采用仅追加的纯文本事件日志记录开发过程中的问题、尝试、修复、决策等，并通过确定性方式投影为紧凑的 AI 可读摘要，结合 Model Context Protocol（MCP）提供给 AI 代理；同时引入预操作门控机制，防止重复错误操作。

Result: 系统完全离线运行，具备不可变日志以支持可审计与可复现的开发流程；在为期两个月、涵盖10个项目的自研研究中验证了207条日志事件的有效性。

Conclusion: projectmem 实现了 Memory-as-Governance 的理念，不仅存储记忆，还主动影响 AI 代理的行为，提升了 AI 辅助开发的效率与可控性。

Abstract: AI coding assistants now support a growing share of software work, from quick scripts to production applications. Yet these agents remain largely stateless: each new session re-reads project files, re-derives prior decisions, and - most costly - may repeat debugging attempts that already failed. Reconstructing this context can consume an estimated 5,000-20,000 tokens per session; the bottleneck is often not model capability but missing project memory. We present projectmem, an open-source, local-first memory and judgment layer for AI coding agents. projectmem records development as an append-only, plain-text event log of typed events - issues, attempts, fixes, decisions, and notes - and deterministically projects that log into compact, AI-readable summaries served through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). Beyond storage, projectmem adds a deterministic pre-action gate that warns an agent before it repeats a previously failed fix or edits a known-fragile file. We frame this as Memory-as-Governance: memory that does not merely answer the agent but acts on its next action. The system runs fully offline with no telemetry; its immutable log also serves as a provenance trail for reproducible, auditable AI-assisted development. projectmem ships as a three-dependency Python package (14 MCP tools, 19 CLI commands, 37 automated tests) and is evaluated through a two-month self-study across 10 projects comprising 207 logged events. Source code: https://github.com/riponcm/projectmem.

</details>


### [233] [Nonslop: A Gamified Experiment in Human-AI Collaborative Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12350)
*Maria Edwards,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过一个游戏化的写作实验，探讨在AI生成建议可用的情况下，人类何时采纳这些建议及其对个体表达的影响。研究设计刻意抑制使用AI建议，以揭示用户真实偏好，并分析不同任务类型、用户行为和回应特征下的人机互动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的迅速发展，人类创造力与个体表达面临挑战。本研究旨在探究在AI辅助创作背景下，人类如何在效率与真实性之间做出选择，特别是在系统明确反对使用AI建议时的行为动机。

Method: 研究采用游戏化写作任务，招募74名参与者（共214条回应），在写作过程中提供AI生成的词语建议。实验设定为反乌托邦情境，AI试图学习残存的人类个性，并通过规则惩罚类似AI的写作风格，从而鼓励参与者避免使用AI建议。收集并分析用户行为数据，包括是否接受AI建议、任务类型、回应长度及风格等变量。

Result: 结果显示，尽管存在规则限制，仍有部分用户选择接受AI建议；用户是否采纳建议受任务难度、个人创造力自我评估及对AI态度等因素影响。分析还发现，接受AI建议的回应更趋同质化，而坚持自主创作的回应展现出更高多样性与个性化特征。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在AI辅助创作中，即使存在抑制机制，人类仍可能倾向于依赖AI建议，这可能削弱个体表达的独特性。游戏化设计有助于揭示真实的人-AI互动偏好，凸显了在追求效率的同时维护创造性自主的重要性。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of large language models (LLMs) raises critical questions about human creativity and individual expression in an era of AI-assisted creation. When do humans adopt AI suggestions, and what are the implications for individual voice?
  This study examines these questions through a gamified writing exercise where 74 participants (214 responses) replied to prompts while AI-generated word suggestions were available as they wrote. The game simulates a dystopian future in which an AI is attempting to learn from what remains of human individuality, and disincentivizes AI-like writing. In doing so, it attempts to create conditions that reveal authentic user preferences rather than default behaviors, such as accepting a readily available AI-generated suggestion. Note that this is a deliberate inversion of the "helpful assistant" design pattern; the system is explicitly forbidding you from accepting AI suggestions.
  We analyze user behavior patterns across different task types, user behaviors, and response characteristics to understand the factors influencing human-AI interaction in creative tasks. The study focuses on when users choose to maintain creative autonomy versus violating the rules of the game and accepting AI assistance. It also explores how these choices relate to response patterns, task characteristics, and user behavior. This gamified approach offers both a framework for studying authentic human-AI interaction and a provocative lens for understanding the tension between efficiency and authenticity in AI-augmented creativity.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [MASK: Multi-Agent Semantic K-Scheduling for Risk-Sensitive 6G Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11249)
*Ahmet Gunhan Aydin,Elif Tugce Ceran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为MASK的多智能体语义调度架构，通过语义重要性评分选择关键智能体进行通信，在带宽受限的情况下实现高效协同控制，并在6G机器人系统中展现出强鲁棒性和抗风险能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在6G协同机器人系统中，无线信道频谱资源有限且被量化为离散资源块，导致所有智能体无法同时传输数据，亟需一种能在严格带宽限制下维持高性能协同控制的机制。

Method: 提出Multi-Agent Semantic K-Scheduling (MASK) 架构，结合Arbiter-Assisted Semantic Information Gating (A-SIG) 机制，基于本地计算的语义重要性分数动态调度前K个最关键的智能体；利用自监督全局编码器聚合优先级观测信息，构建紧凑潜在状态，支持分布策略应对尾部风险。

Result: 在多个基准测试中验证，MASK在仅允许小部分智能体接入信道的情况下，性能仍可媲美无通信限制的基线方法，并表现出对数据包擦除的内在鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MASK通过语义感知的调度机制有效缓解了频谱资源紧张与协同控制性能之间的矛盾，证明了语义调度是实现资源受限6G协同系统的关键使能技术。

Abstract: Realizing the vision of 6G connected robotics requires reconciling high-performance collaborative control with the rigid spectral limitations of physical wireless channels. In realistic collaborative sensing scenarios, spectral resources are quantized into finite physical resource blocks or orthogonal subcarriers, rendering simultaneous transmission by all agents infeasible. To address this, we propose Multi-Agent Semantic K-Scheduling (MASK), a control architecture designed to sustain robust, risk-aware coordination under strict instantaneous bandwidth caps. We introduce Arbiter-Assisted Semantic Information Gating (A-SIG), a lightweight coordination mechanism that enforces hard access constraints by scheduling only the top-K agents based on locally computed semantic importance scores. By aggregating these prioritized observations into a compact latent state, a self-supervised global encoder enables a distributional policy to mitigate tail risks despite data sparsity. We evaluate MASK across diverse benchmarks, demonstrating that it matches the performance of communication-unconstrained baselines even when channel access is restricted to a small fraction of the swarm size. Furthermore, the framework exhibits inherent resilience to packet erasures, validating semantic scheduling as a critical enabler for resource-constrained 6G systems.

</details>


### [235] [Model-based Optimization of Anguilliform Swimming Gaits for Soft Robotic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11278)
*Brian Van Stratum,James Gallentine,Caleb Rucker,Eric Barth,Jonathan E. Clark,Kourosh Shoele*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了软体仿生双环境机器人SLIDER及其建模与优化方法，实现了在游泳和攀爬任务中的协同优化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一种能够在水下和陆地环境中高效运动的多功能仿生机器人，并通过建模与优化提升其性能。

Method: 采用Lighthill的大振幅细长体理论模拟流体作用力，建立快速非线性结构模型并结合遗传算法对控制模式和尾鳍设计进行协同优化。

Result: 实验测得SLIDER在静水中达到21.7 +/- 0.4 cm/s（0.59体长/秒）的游动速度，发现低频游动受阻力主导，高频则受惯性力影响更大。

Conclusion: 所提出的建模与优化框架有效支持了多模态机器人的设计，在兼顾攀爬形态的同时实现了高效的游泳性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the Soft Lamprey-Inspired Dual Environment Robot (SLIDER) and a proper modeling and optimization procedure employed to design the robot. We represent the primary fluid environment actions - inertial effects, vortex forces, and viscous dissipation - using Lighthill's theory for large-amplitude elongated bodies. For structural design parameters such as internal pressure, tail size, and body stiffness, a fast, geometrically and materially nonlinear model is developed and validated. The fluid-structure interaction equations are solved implicitly with an efficient second-order box method. A pneumatic manifold robotic system is employed to actuate SLIDER in a quiescent water tank environment, allowing cross-comparison of computational and experimental results. We find that low-frequency swimming is dominated by resistant environmental forces, whereas higher-frequency swimming is primarily affected by inertial fluid forces. Using our efficient model alongside a genetic algorithm, we co-optimize a swimming control pattern and caudal fin design (subject to SLIDER's climbing morphology) to achieve a tethered swimming speed of 21.7 +/- 0.4 cm/s (0.59 Bl/s). Furthermore, we investigate the optimization procedure for a multimodal robot performing both swimming and climbing tasks.

</details>


### [236] [Embodied-R1.5: Evolving Physical Intelligence via Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11324)
*Yifu Yuan,Yaoting Huang,Xianze Yao,Yutong Li,Shuoheng Zhang,Linqi Han,Pengyi Li,Jiangeng Sun,Wenting Jia,Zhao Zhang,Yuhao Liu,Ruihao Liao,Yucheng Hu,Qiyu Wu,Yuxiao Li,Zibin Dong,Fei Ni,Yan Zheng,Shuyang Gu,Yi Ma,Hongyao Tang,Han Hu,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Embodied-R1.5，一种统一的具身基础模型（EFM），集成了具身认知、任务规划、修正和指向等多种能力，通过大规模数据和多任务强化学习策略，在仅8B参数下在24个具身视觉语言模型基准中实现了16项SOTA，并可微调为高性能VLA模型，验证了其在真实机器人上的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现通用物理智能，需要一个能够整合多种具身推理能力的统一模型架构，同时解决现有模型在数据覆盖、任务冲突和长视野任务执行中的局限性。

Method: 构建了三个自动化数据构建管道，生成超过150亿token的大规模数据系统；设计了多任务平衡的强化学习训练策略；提出Planner-Grounder-Corrector (PGC)闭环框架，使单个模型能自主执行并自我修正长视野任务。

Result: 在24个具身VLM基准中，Embodied-R1.5在16个上达到SOTA，超越Gemini-Robotics-ER-1.5和GPT-5.4；微调后在4个主流操作基准上优于π₀.₅等先进VLA模型；在真实机器人上成功完成指令跟随、功能定位、关节物体操作和复杂长视野任务的零样本测试。

Conclusion: Embodied-R1.5是一种高效且通用的具身基础模型，具备强大的内部具身能力，可通过少量数据微调迁移至具体控制任务，并展现出优异的物理世界泛化性能，推动了通用物理智能的发展。

Abstract: We introduce Embodied-R1.5, a unified Embodied Foundation Model (EFM) that integrates comprehensive embodied reasoning capabilities, spanning embodied cognition, task planning, correction, and pointing, within a single architecture toward general physical intelligence. Leveraging three automated data construction pipelines to significantly expand the data coverage of critical capabilities, we build a large-scale data system of over 15B tokens, and design a multi-task balanced RL recipe to alleviate heterogeneous task conflicts. We further introduce a Planner-Grounder-Corrector (PGC) closed-loop framework that enables a single model to autonomously execute and self-correct over long-horizon tasks. With only 8B parameters, Embodied-R1.5 achieves SOTA on 16 out of 24 embodied VLM benchmarks, surpassing leading models like Gemini-Robotics-ER-1.5 and GPT-5.4. Benefiting from the internalized embodied capabilities, Embodied-R1.5 can be fine-tuned into a VLA with only a small amount of data, outperforming leading VLA models like $π_{0.5}$ across 4 popular manipulation benchmark suites. We further conduct extensive zero-shot real-robot experiments, validating performance in instruction following, affordance grounding, articulated object manipulation, and long-horizon complex tasks, demonstrating strong generalization to the physical world. We open-source model weights, datasets, training code, and EmbodiedEvalKit, an evaluation framework tailored for embodied tasks, to facilitate future research in EFMs.

</details>


### [237] [HiPi: Reproducible High-Fidelity Piezoresistive Sensors for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11372)
*Changyi Lin,Raihan Haque,Hui-Ping Wang,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiPi是一种可复现的高保真压阻式触觉传感系统，通过优化硬件架构和制造工艺，在保证高保真度的同时提升了可复制性与可扩展性，适用于双手机器人操作和多指机器人系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压阻式触觉传感器在可复现性和高保真读出之间存在权衡：易复制的设计往往牺牲性能，而高性能系统又难以制造和部署。本文旨在构建一个兼具高保真、可复现、易部署且支持多传感器扩展的系统。

Method: 基于低串扰读出原理，重新设计整个硬件堆栈，包括兼容商用PCB制造的紧凑型读出电路板、基于STM32的小型低成本MCU模块、优化的通信流程以实现220Hz高速读出，以及使用FPCB的导电层简化传感器制作与堆叠。

Result: 实验显示HiPi显著优于可复现基线方法，在四块密集触觉阵列（共2048个taxel）的双手机器人设置下实现220Hz读出；结构化3D打印接触模式测试中，平均IoU从0.428提升至0.797，Dice系数从0.539提升至0.886。

Conclusion: HiPi有效弥合了可复现制造与高保真读出之间的鸿沟，使高密度压阻式触觉传感在多指和双手机器人操作中更具实用性。

Abstract: Piezoresistive tactile sensors are attractive for robotic manipulation because they are thin, lightweight, low-cost, and scalable to dense large-area sensing. However, existing systems still face a practical trade-off: recent reproducible designs emphasize accessibility and ease of reproduction, whereas high-fidelity readout architectures remain more difficult to fabricate, assemble, and deploy. We present HiPi, a reproducible high-fidelity piezoresistive sensing system for robotic manipulation. Building on a low-crosstalk readout principle, HiPi redesigns the complete hardware stack around reproducibility, deployability, and multi-sensor scalability. The system includes a compact readout PCB compatible with commercial PCB fabrication and assembly services, eliminating manual soldering; a smaller and lower-cost STM32-based MCU module; an optimized communication pipeline that achieves 220 Hz readout in a bimanual setup with four dense tactile arrays (2048 taxels in total); and FPCB-based conductive layers that simplify sensor fabrication and stacking. Experiments with structured 3D-printed contact patterns show that HiPi preserves contact geometry substantially better than a reproducible baseline, improving the average IoU from 0.428 to 0.797 and the average Dice score from 0.539 to 0.886. These results suggest that HiPi bridges an important gap between reproducible fabrication and high-fidelity readout, making dense piezoresistive tactile sensing more practical for bimanual manipulation and multi-fingered robotic systems.

</details>


### [238] [PLUME: Probabilistic Latent Unified World Modeling and Parameter Estimation for Multi-Finger Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11396)
*Abhinav Kumar,Soshi Iba,Rana Soltani Zarrin,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为PLUME的联合学习方法，通过在线参数推断实现仿真到现实的零样本迁移，在多指灵巧操作任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作对物理参数敏感，而实际部署时这些参数未知，传统域随机化不足以应对精确操作任务的需求。

Method: 提出Probabilistic Latent Unified world Modeling and parameter Estimation (PLUME)，学习一个联合表示多种物理参数及奖励的潜在空间，并在该框架下联合建模系统动力学与参数信念演化，支持基于在线参数推断的规划。

Result: 在模拟的螺丝刀旋转、阀门旋转、提桶和弹盘任务以及真实的螺丝刀旋转硬件任务上验证了方法的有效性，实现了成功的零样本迁移，并优于现有的离线强化学习和行为克隆基线方法。

Conclusion: PLUME能够有效对齐真实动力学，无需重新训练或微调，为仿真到现实的灵巧操作提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation with multi-finger hands can be sensitive to physical parameters such as object shape, pose, and friction coefficients. While simulation enables large-scale data collection with known parameter values, simulation-trained policies must still handle uncertainty at deployment, where the true parameters and therefore the true dynamics are unknown. Standard domain randomization strategies may be insufficient for precise tasks like screwdriver turning, as manipulation strategies may need to change depending on specific parameter values. To address this, we propose Probabilistic Latent Unified world Modeling and parameter Estimation (PLUME), a world model that jointly learns to evolve a belief over parameter values as well as the system dynamics conditioned on those parameters. We learn a latent space to jointly represent multiple qualitatively different physical parameters along with rewards, themselves functions of partially-observable variables, to inform planning. Our novel learning framework leads to efficient alignment of the world model to true dynamics through online parameter inference as opposed to re-training or fine-tuning. We evaluate our method on simulated screwdriver turning, valve turning, bucket lifting, and disk flicking tasks, as well as a hardware screwdriver turning task, where we achieve successful zero-shot transfer of our simulation-trained policy and outperform state-of-the-art offline reinforcement learning and world-model-augmented behavior cloning baselines. Please see our website at https://plume-world-model.github.io for videos.

</details>


### [239] [Dynamic Execution Horizon Prediction for Chunk-based Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11408)
*Yuchi Zhao,Miroslav Bogdanovic,Arjun Sohal,Liyu Tao,Kourosh Darvish,Alán Aspuru-Guzik,Florian Shkurti,Animesh Garg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态执行视界预测（DEHP）方法，用于改进机器人策略中的动作分块执行，通过在线强化学习训练一个轻量级分支来预测执行步数，从而在保持预训练策略冻结的同时提升精细操作任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动作分块策略依赖固定的执行视界，在需要频繁重规划的精细操作任务中表现不佳，且执行视界的选取通常依赖经验调参，缺乏自适应性。

Method: 提出动态执行视界预测（DEHP），训练一个轻量级的执行视界预测分支，使用在线强化学习进行优化，同时保持原有的预训练分块策略不变，实现对执行长度的自适应调整。

Result: DEHP在多种高精度、长视野的操作任务中显著提升了成功率；定性分析表明其能在精细操作阶段预测较短视界，在自由空间运动时预测较长视界。

Conclusion: DEHP有效平衡了开环分块执行的效率与闭环单步控制的反应性，兼容黑箱策略模型，为动作分块提供了更灵活、自适应的执行机制。

Abstract: Action chunking has become a standard design in modern robot policies, from diffusion/flow policies to vision-language-action models, where the policy predicts a sequence of actions and executes a fixed number of them instead of acting one step at a time. However, this paradigm relies on a key assumption: a fixed execution horizon. During chunk execution, the policy operates open-loop, which is particularly problematic for fine-grained manipulation tasks that require frequent replanning. In practice, the execution horizon is typically chosen through empirical tuning and is highly task-dependent. To this end, we propose Dynamic Execution Horizon Prediction (DEHP), an effective method that trains a lightweight execution-horizon prediction branch using online reinforcement learning while keeping the pretrained chunk policy completely frozen. This makes the method compatible with black-box chunk policies and isolates the effect of adapting the execution horizon from changes to the underlying action generator. Across our evaluations, DEHP improves the success rate of different high-precision and long-horizon manipulation tasks by a large margin. Our qualitative analysis further shows that DEHP predicts shorter execution horizons during fine-grained stages of the task and longer horizons during free-space motion. In this way, DEHP balances the efficiency of open-loop chunk execution with the reactivity of closed-loop single-step control. Project page: https://dehp-chunking.github.io/

</details>


### [240] [A Modular Dual-Camera Pipeline for Micro-Inspection Using Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11419)
*S. H. Mirtajadini,N. Rublein,R. M. Ramakrishnan,G. ter Maat,M. Aldibaja,A. Y. Mersha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为aerial_micro_inspection的通用空中微检视管道，利用双相机无人机系统实现远距离高精度检测，适用于树木和温室等多种场景，并在仿真和实际实验中验证了其鲁棒性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机检测系统需近距离飞行或复杂路径规划，易受扰动和定位误差影响，且依赖目标先验信息，难以适用于非结构化目标（如树木、车辆、行人）的微小细节检测。

Method: 提出aerial_micro_inspection框架，使用PX4无人机搭载双相机：一个云台变焦检测相机用于远距离捕捉细节，一个广角立体导航相机用于现场表面感知、测距与区域划分；通过视觉反馈回路补偿无人机运动，逐区进行微检视。

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法在扰动下具有更高的覆盖鲁棒性；真实实验成功应用于橡树毒蛾及其卵的检测和温室粘虫板上的粉虱识别，实现了高细节成像与有效检测。

Conclusion: 该开源管道基于ROS 2开发，具有良好的通用性和可扩展性，可通过更换表面分割与目标检测模块适配新应用场景。

Abstract: Most existing drone-based inspection systems require the drone to fly dangerously close to the target or follow complex flight paths to capture small details. In addition, drone flight is affected by disturbances and localization inaccuracies, which can cause the drone to lose sight of its supposed target when it has a narrow view. Furthermore, trajectory planning often requires prior information about the target's geometry, position, and orientation, which is not always available for non-structural targets such as trees, vehicles, or people. To address these challenges, this paper presents aerial_micro_inspection, a generic pipeline for aerial micro-inspection across different use cases. The pipeline assumes a PX4-powered drone equipped with two cameras: (i) a zoomed, gimbal-mounted inspection camera that captures fine details without requiring the drone to fly very close to the target, and (ii) a wide-field-of-view stereo navigation camera that acquires the target surface on site, estimates its range, and partitions it into smaller inspection regions. In addition, a vision-based feedback loop compensates for drone motion while the inspection camera visits small partitions of a larger surface. We evaluate the pipeline in simulation and real-world experiments, mainly in two use-case scenarios: tree inspection for detecting oak processionary caterpillars and their eggs, and greenhouse inspection of sticky traps for detecting whiteflies. The results show improved coverage robustness under drone disturbances in simulation, as well as effective detection of caterpillars and eggs and high-detail imaging of insects in real-world experiments. The pipeline is open-source, developed in ROS 2, and can be adapted to new applications by replacing the surface-segmentation and micro-target detection checkpoints. The code is available at: https://github.com/SaxionMechatronics/aerial_micro_inspection

</details>


### [241] [Bridging the sim2real gap in the table tennis robot with a transformer-based ball states predictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11464)
*Yin Bi,Christian Conti,Bilan Yang,Alexander Sigrist,Peter Dürr,Naoya Takahashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Transformer的框架用于乒乓球状态预测，并结合大规模真实数据实现准确的长时域预测，通过部署阶段替换预测器（SPAD）策略有效缩小了仿真到现实的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理模型方法依赖精确参数和初始状态，而学习方法难以捕捉长期时序依赖且训练数据有限，因此需要一种能利用真实世界数据、准确建模长期依赖的预测方法。

Method: 采用Transformer架构利用注意力机制从历史观测中直接建模长程时间相关性，不依赖显式的飞行或弹跳模型；收集了来自不同水平玩家和多种发球机配置的大规模真实世界数据集；提出SPAD策略，在部署时用真实世界训练的预测器替代训练时的物理模拟器。

Result: 所提方法实现了高精度的长时域预测，SPAD策略显著缩小了仿真与现实之间的差距，同时保持了基于仿真的训练效率和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的预测框架结合真实数据与SPAD策略，为机器人乒乓球中的高速动态控制提供了高效且可泛化的解决方案，推动了仿真到现实迁移的实际应用。

Abstract: Robotic table tennis is a representative benchmark for high-speed, closed-loop robotic control in dynamic environments, where accurate and fast prediction of ball states is critical for reliable planning and control. Physics-based approaches rely heavily on accurate parameter identification and precise initial state, while learning-based methods often struggle to capture long-range temporal dependencies and are typically trained on limited or simulated data. We propose a transformer-based framework for table tennis ball state prediction that leverages attention mechanisms to model long-range temporal correlations directly from historical observations, without relying on explicit flight or bounce models. To support robust learning and generalization, we collected a large-scale real-world dataset from players of varying skill levels and diverse ball cannon configurations. The combination of a high-capacity transformer architecture and extensive real-world data enables accurate long-horizon forecasting. Building on this capability, we introduce a plug-and-play sim-to-real transfer strategy, Swap Predictor at Deployment (SPAD), which replaces the physics-based simulator used during training with the proposed real-world-trained predictor at deployment, improving the sim-to-real transferability of the policy without requiring retraining. We demonstrate that this simple substitution effectively narrows the sim-to-real gap while preserving the efficiency and scalability of simulation-based training.

</details>


### [242] [Steering Multirobot Behavior via Closed-Loop Affine Activation Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11489)
*Satyajeet Das,Darren Chiu,Shashank Hegde,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLAE是一种在推理时通过编辑中间激活来引导冻结策略行为的框架，能够在保持基础策略性能的同时实现机器人行为的在线自适应调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的机器人需要在其预训练策略范围之外调整行为，但微调或重新训练可能导致灾难性遗忘，因此需要一种不改变原有策略权重的行为调节方法。

Method: 提出CLAE（闭环仿射激活编辑）框架，通过对冻结策略的中间激活训练稀疏自编码器，利用事后探测选择与行为相关的潜在特征，并使用轻量级基于强化学习的控制策略在推理过程中对选定的潜在变量施加状态依赖的仿射编辑。

Result: 在多四旋翼无人机导航任务中验证了CLAE的有效性，能够调节单个机器人的速度曲线、维持多机编队以及使机器人减少在监控环境中的暴露。

Conclusion: CLAE能够在不影响原始策略性能的前提下，在推理阶段灵活地引导冻结策略产生新行为，适用于动态环境下的单机与多机协同任务。

Abstract: Real-world robots need to adapt their behavior beyond the envelope of their pre-trained policy. Policy finetuning or retraining are options, but they risk catastrophic forgetting, degrading the pretrained policy's base performance. To combat this, we introduce CLAE: Closed-Loop Affine Activation Editing, an inference-time framework for steering the behavior of a frozen policy by editing intermediate activations while keeping the base policy weights and downstream action head untouched. CLAE approaches behavior steering as a closed-loop problem whose outputs edit policy activations that adapt online to the robot state, environment, target behavior, and multi-robot context. It trains a sparse autoencoder over frozen-policy activations, selects behavior-relevant latent features via post-hoc probing, and learns a lightweight RL-based steering policy that applies state-dependent affine edits to selected latents during inference. We validate CLAE on a frozen multi-quadrotor navigation policy trained to perform a single task: navigating robots to a set of goal locations while avoiding obstacles. Through extensive simulations and physical tests, we show that while navigating to their goal positions, CLAE can 1. steer individual robot behavior by controlling each robot's velocity profile; 2. coordinate multirobot behavior by preserving a desired formation; and 3. produce entirely new behavior wherein robots are required to reduce their exposure to surveillance cameras in the environment.

</details>


### [243] [Learning Object Manipulation from Scratch via Contrastive Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11525)
*Tongle Shen,Caleb Chuck,Fan Feng,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Interaction-weighted Resampling (IWR) 的新方法，用于改进目标条件下的机器人操作任务中的对比强化学习（CRL），通过在交互前后进行交互感知重采样，提升表示学习和规划能力，在仿真和真实世界中均显著提高了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CRL方法在涉及丰富交互的操作任务中表现不佳，主要因为物体中心的交互（如接触或抓取）会引起动态模式的突变，导致标准CRL难以建模分段非线性的可达性结构。

Method: 将操作动力学建模为分段光滑马尔可夫过程，并引入IWR方法，在交互前、中、后阶段进行交互感知的重采样，以保留决定未来可达性的模式边界，从而更好地捕捉多模态和分段非线性结构。

Result: 在多个交互密集型环境中（包括2D控制、机械臂操作和机器人冰球），IWR相比现有CRL方法平均提升19.8%；并通过sim-to-real成功实现首个能在真实世界击中目标的机器人冰球代理，任务成功率从25%提升至60%。

Conclusion: IWR通过显式建模交互引起的模式变化，有效解决了CRL在复杂操作任务中的局限性，显著提升了学习效率和实际应用性能。

Abstract: Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (CRL) has seen recent success in a wide variety of goal-conditioned robotics tasks by learning structured representations of the dynamics. However, despite its success in locomotion and simpler control domains, CRL often struggles in interaction-rich manipulation. We argue that a key source of this difficulty is object-centric interaction, such as contact or grasping, that induces distinct changes in the underlying dynamic modes. In this work, we formulate manipulation dynamics as a piecewise-smooth Markov process and show that interaction-induced mode changes create piecewise nonlinear reachability structures that are difficult for standard CRL energy functions to represent and plan over. Based on this analysis, we introduce Interaction-weighted Resampling (IWR). IWR performs interaction-aware resampling around phases before, during, and after interactions, encouraging the learned representation to preserve the mode boundaries that determine future reachability to capture multi-modal and piecewise nonlinear reachability. Across interaction-centric environments, including 2D dynamic control, robotic manipulation, and robot air hockey, IWR improves both sample efficiency and overall performance over prior CRL methods, with 19.8% average improvement in simulation. Finally, using a sim-to-real pipeline with policies trained by IWR, we demonstrate the first real-world goal-conditioned robot air hockey agent capable of hitting goals, improving success from 25% to 60%. Project Page: IWR-arxiv.github.io.

</details>


### [244] [Adversarial Attacks on Learned Policies for Surgical Robotic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11535)
*Shutong Jin,Ziyang Chen,Preethi Satish,Paavan Gupta,Florian T. Pokorny,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了学习型策略在手术机器人中的对抗性威胁，提出了两种攻击模式：干扰攻击和引导攻击，并设计了三种对抗攻击方法。通过560次物理实验验证，现有先进策略的成功率平均下降61%，表明视觉对抗攻击可能严重影响手术安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于学习的策略被用于增强机器人辅助手术中医生的操作能力，其安全性问题日益突出。本文旨在探究从视觉输入到机器人动作的端到端映射是否容易受到对抗性攻击，从而可能导致患者受伤。

Method: 研究提出两种威胁模式：干扰攻击和引导攻击；设计了三种具有不同信息访问级别的对抗攻击方法；在去皮和缝合两个外科子任务上评估ACT、Diffusion Policy和Pi0三种端到端策略架构的鲁棒性；引入一类新的光度对抗攻击，模拟光照变化等自然视觉改变以提高攻击的视觉合理性。

Result: 560次使用模型进行的物理实验表明，最先进的策略可被显著干扰，外科子任务成功率平均降低61%；新提出的光度攻击在保持视觉合理的同时仍具强有效性。

Conclusion: 学习型手术机器人策略易受视觉对抗攻击，当前模型存在严重安全隐患，需在未来研究中加强防御机制以保障临床应用安全。

Abstract: Learning-based policies are being considered to augment the dexterity of human surgeons in robot-assisted surgery. Can the end-to-end mapping from visual observations to robot actions be vulnerable to adversarial attacks, potentially leading to patient injury? In this paper, we present the first study of adversarial threats to learning-based policies in surgical robotics. We investigate two threat modes: (a) disruptive attacks, where imperceptible visual perturbations interrupt policy execution, and (b) steering attacks, where such perturbations steer policy actions toward attacker-specified directions. We formulate three adversarial attack methods, each with increasing access to policy information, and evaluate their impact on two surgical subtasks: debridement and suturing. Our evaluation covers three end-to-end policy architectures: ACT, Diffusion Policy, and Pi0. In addition, we introduce a new class of photometric adversarial attacks that mimic natural visual changes, such as lighting variations, to generate effective yet visually plausible perturbations. Results from 560 physical experiments using phantoms for debridement and suturing suggest that state-of-the-art policies can be significantly disrupted, resulting in an average 61% reduction in surgical subtask success rates. Project page: https://sites.google.com/view/adversary-surgery

</details>


### [245] [ConsistencyPlanner: Real-time Planning with Fast-Sampling Consistency Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11569)
*Qichao Zhang,Xing Fang,Jiaqi Fang,Zhenwen Cai,Jie Ling,Qiankun Yu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Consistency Planner的实时规划框架，利用快速采样一致性模型和注意力增强解码器，在复杂驾驶场景中实现高效、安全的多模态行为建模与决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则的方法缺乏适应动态交通环境的能力，而现有学习-based方法难以在多模态行为建模与实时规划之间取得平衡。

Method: 采用快速采样一致性模型生成多样化的未来轨迹，并设计注意力增强解码器动态融合异构输入特征（如场景特征和动作标记）。

Result: 在Waymax仿真器中的实验表明，该方法在安全性指标上优于现有方法，尤其在具有挑战性的动态场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: Consistency Planner通过高效的多模态采样和异构特征融合，实现了实时且安全的闭环规划，适用于复杂的现实驾驶环境。

Abstract: Closed-loop planning in complex, real-world driving scenarios presents a critical challenge for autonomous driving systems. While traditional rule-based methods are interpretable, their predefined heuristics lack the adaptability for dynamic traffic environments. Learning-based approaches have shown considerable promise. Conversely, learning-based approaches, despite their promise, struggle to balance the modeling diverse and multimodal driving behaviors and real-time planning, often leading to indecisive or unsafe actions. To address this limitation, we propose Consistency Planner, a real-time planning framework with fast-sampling consistency models. Our approach is built upon two key technical contributions. Efficient Multimodal Sampling: We employ fast-sampling consistency models to generate a diverse set of plausible future trajectories. This enables efficient, real-time exploration of multimodal actions, overcoming the computational bottlenecks of previous iterative generative methods. Heterogeneous Feature Fusion: We introduce an attention-enhanced decoder that dynamically integrates heterogeneous input features (including scene feature and action token) into a cohesive representation for robust planning. Extensive evaluation in the Waymax simulator demonstrates superior performance in safety metrics compared to existing methods, with particularly strong results in challenging dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [246] [Distortion-Resilient Robotic Imitation Learning for Autonomous Cable Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11577)
*Hao Wang,Fu-Zhao Ou,Shiqi Wang,Zhaolin Wan,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的机器人模仿学习框架，通过图像质量评估模块和基于置信度的学习机制，在存在图像信号失真的情况下提升决策模块的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能控制方法在图像观测失真时表现不佳，且未充分利用图像质量信息，缺乏针对此类场景的专用解决方案。

Method: 设计了一个包含图像质量评估模块、基于置信度的学习机制和决策模块的模仿学习框架；前者提取图像质量信息，中者自适应地优先处理难样本，后者输出技能或动作。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提框架能有效提升决策模块在低质量图像输入下的整体性能。

Conclusion: 该框架通过融合图像质量感知与置信度驱动学习，增强了机器人在图像失真场景下的控制鲁棒性和学习效率。

Abstract: The rapid development of intelligent control methodologies has endowed robots with powerful autonomous intelligence. Cable routing, a ubiquitous foundational task in industry, provides a rigorous benchmark for robotic dexterity and sequential decision-making. In these practical scenarios, image observation distortion frequently occurs. Samples characterized by low-quality image observations often hinder accurate model training, posing challenges to the reliability and accuracy of intelligent control systems. Nevertheless, no dedicated intelligent control solution has been proposed for scenarios of image signal distortion. Meanwhile, image quality information has not been sufficiently exploited to further enhance the performance of intelligent control methodologies. To this end, we propose a novel robotic imitation learning framework that comprises an image quality assessment module, a confidence-based learning mechanism, and a decision-making module, which is designed to maintain high performance even under distorted image observations. In the proposed framework, the image quality assessment module synergizes with the confidence-based learning mechanism to enhance the efficacy of the decision-making module. Specifically, the image quality assessment module is incorporated to extract image quality information from image observations, while the confidence-based learning mechanism adaptively prioritizes challenging samples to improve learning effectiveness. The decision-making module determines appropriate discrete skills or continuous actions. Experimental results demonstrate that our formulated framework enhances the overall performance of the decision-making module.

</details>


### [247] [LUCID: Learning Embodiment-Agnostic Intent Models from Unstructured Human Videos for Scalable Dexterous Robot Skill Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11628)
*Harsh Gupta,Guanya Shi,Wenzhen Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LUCID是一种从互联网规模的非结构化人类视频中学习任务意图，并在大规模并行仿真中学习机器人控制的两阶段框架，实现了跨不同机器人的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人学习方法依赖昂贵且特定于实体的演示数据，而非结构化的人类视频提供了更可扩展的数据来源，但缺乏与机器人动作的直接关联。

Method: 提出LUCID框架：第一阶段利用非结构化人类视频训练意图模型以预测短期场景变化；第二阶段在仿真中训练具身感知运动策略将意图转化为具体动作；意图接口在不同控制器间共享，支持多实体应用。

Result: 在五个真实世界操作任务上验证了LUCID的有效性，包括仅由互联网视频监督的搅拌、擦拭和分拣任务（实现对新场景和物体实例的零样本迁移），以及由自采集智能手机视频监督的推移和布线任务。

Conclusion: LUCID能够有效利用非结构化人类视频学习任务意图，并通过共享意图接口实现跨不同机器人实体的泛化控制，展现出强大的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: The most widely-adopted robot learning pipelines today learn skills from robot demonstrations or structured human data, which are expensive to collect and tied to specific embodiments. In contrast, unstructured human videos provide a scalable alternative. They contain diverse manipulation demonstrations across objects, scenes, and strategies, but are not directly connected to robot action. We propose LUCID, a two-stage framework that learns task intent from unstructured human videos drawn from internet-scale datasets and learns robot control in massively-parallel simulation. The intent model predicts short-horizon intent (what should happen next in the scene) from the current observation in closed loop. An embodiment-specific sensorimotor policy converts this intent into robot actions. The intent interface is shared across controllers, so the same intent model can be applied to different embodiments, from our primary dexterous hand to a parallel-jaw gripper. We evaluate LUCID on five real-world manipulation tasks: stirring, wiping, and binning supervised by only internet video, with zero-shot transfer to novel scenes and object instances; and push-T and cable routing supervised by 1 hr each of self-collected smartphone video. Project page: https://lucid-robot.github.io/.

</details>


### [248] [SAFER-Nav: Enhancing Safety for Visual Robot Navigation via Segmentation-Aware Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11636)
*Geonyeong Ko,Giung Lee,Changjoo Nam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种新的导航模型，通过微调将障碍物边界和可通行空间结构直接融入策略，兼容多种基于RGB的骨干网络，在多平台和环境中减少碰撞频率同时保持目标到达性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的导航模型在陌生环境或变化条件下泛化能力差，轨迹虽目标导向但不安全，且缺乏对障碍物边界和自由空间的显式表征。

Method: 通过微调将障碍物边界和可通行空间结构直接引入导航策略，设计兼容多种RGB-based backbone的模型。

Result: 在多个机器人平台、室内环境及静态动态障碍场景下，相比ViNT、NoMaD及其CARE变体，碰撞频率降低，同时保持了目标到达能力。

Conclusion: 该方法通过显式建模环境结构显著提升了视觉导航策略的安全性与泛化能力，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Vision-based navigation models, particularly foundation models, generate viable trajectories from RGB observations alone. However, even state-of-the-art transformer- and diffusion-based policies struggle to generalize in unfamiliar deployment environments containing unseen obstacles or shifted conditions. The resulting trajectories often remain goal-directed but unsafe. Existing efforts improve safety through external trajectory correction or internal geometric priors, yet the resulting policies are not trained to explicitly represent obstacle boundaries or traversable free-space structure. To address this, we propose a navigation model that incorporates these structures directly into the policy via fine-tuning and is designed to be compatible with diverse RGB-based backbones. Across multiple robot platforms, indoor environments, and static and dynamic obstacle scenarios, our method reduces collision frequency relative to ViNT, NoMaD, and their CARE-augmented variants while maintaining goal-reaching performance.

</details>


### [249] [Improving Human Diving Endurance with a Field-Deployable, Untethered Exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11704)
*Zhihao Zhou,Zhenmeng Ju,Rui Yang,Chenxi Zhang,Zhihao Zhou,Ming Xu,Enhao Zheng,Dongjie Jiang,Lecheng Ruan,Jingeng Mai,Qining Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiveMate是一种可部署于真实水下环境的无系绳外骨骼，通过自适应踢腿辅助显著提升潜水耐力，延长潜水距离和时间，并降低生理消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下运动受限于高能耗和有限的呼吸气体供应，现有外骨骼技术尚未探索用于增强潜水耐力的可能性。

Method: 开发名为DiveMate的无系绳、可野外部署的外骨骼系统，在自然潜水环境中提供自适应踢腿辅助，并评估其对气体消耗率、肌肉激活和运动学特征的影响。

Result: 使用DiveMate后，相同能量（呼吸气体）下的航行距离增加42.9%，潜水时长延长54.9%，净气体消耗率降低47.0%，肌肉激活显著减少，运动效率提高。

Conclusion: 外骨骼辅助能有效提升人类潜水耐力，拓展了外骨骼的应用边界，为未来水下辅助设备的设计与评估提供了参考。

Abstract: Human endurance in underwater locomotion is fundamentally restricted by high energetic demands to overcome drag and the finite supply of self-contained breathing gas. While exoskeleton technology can reduce the metabolic cost of humans in terrestrial locomotion, its potential to enhance human endurance during underwater diving remains entirely unexplored. Here, we present DiveMate, a field-deployable, untethered exoskeleton designed to improve human diving endurance via adaptive kick assistance in real-world underwater environments. During naturalistic diving, DiveMate increases the travel distance using a given energy (breathing gas) by 42.9% and extends dive duration by 54.9% through reducing gas consumption rate. Marked reductions in muscle activation indicate a decrease in physiological exertion, with the net gas consumption rate decreasing by 47.0%. Kinematic characteristics and regularity improvements further underpin efficient energy economy. These results suggest that applying exoskeleton assistance is beneficial for improving human diving endurance and augmenting their ability to explore the aquatic world. This study extends the application frontier of exoskeletons and provides a potential reference for the design and assessment of future underwater assistive devices.

</details>


### [250] [Explore From Sketch: Accelerating UAV Exploration in Large-scale Environments with Prior Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11708)
*Tiancheng Lai,Yuman Gao,Xiangyu Li,Ruitian Pang,Xingpeng Wang,Siqi Shen,Mengke Zhang,Yin He,Fei Gao,Chao Xu,Yanjun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用稀疏、未对齐甚至存在差异的2D先验地图进行LiDAR无人机探索的新框架，通过鲁棒的2D-3D点云配准和分层视点规划策略，在大规模复杂环境中显著提升了探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模拓扑复杂的环境中，无人机自主探索常因调度次优和路径绕行导致效率低下；而尽管现有先验地图（如施工图）通常不精确，但广泛可用且具备提供全局结构引导的潜力。

Method: 提出一种鲁棒的2D-3D点云配准流程，结合GeoContext描述子、多帧验证机制和Scale-ICP算法实现配准，并设计分层视点规划策略，采用蒙特卡洛树搜索与风险感知选择器在定位不确定性下生成高效路径。

Result: 基准测试显示探索效率提升高达34.2%，飞行距离减少37.9%，仿真与实地实验验证了方法对先验地图不完整性和形变的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效利用不完美的先验地图提升无人机在复杂环境中的探索效率和路径优化，具有较强的实用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration with UAVs in large-scale, topologically complex environments often suffers from low efficiency due to suboptimal scheduling and detours. Prior maps (e.g., construction drawings), although usually imprecise and flawed, are readily available in many scenarios and have the potential to provide global structural guidance. This paper presents a novel exploration framework that leverages sparse, unaligned, and even discrepant 2D prior maps for LiDAR-based UAV exploration. First, a robust 2D-3D point cloud registration pipeline is proposed to align LiDAR observations with prior maps. The registration pipeline combines a GeoContext descriptor for single-frame candidate retrieval, a multi-frame verification mechanism for coarse transformation estimation with outlier rejection, and a Scale-ICP algorithm for refinement. The registration module can handle map discrepancies and provide multiple hypotheses when geometric ambiguities arise. To effectively utilize the registration results for exploration planning, we further develop a hierarchical viewpoint planning strategy under localization uncertainties. The hierarchical strategy first spatially attaches local viewpoints to prior guidepoints and adopts a Monte Carlo Tree Search solver to determine their traversal sequence under each registration hypothesis. To mitigate registration uncertainty, a risk-aware selector evaluates prior sequences using confidence-weighted travel risk, and a fixed-endpoint traveling salesman problem is formulated to generate an efficient local coverage path under the selected prior guidance. Benchmark evaluations reveal up to 34.2% improvement in exploration efficiency and 37.9% reduction in flight distance compared to state-of-the-art methods, while extensive simulations and field experiments further demonstrate robustness to prior map incompleteness and deformations.

</details>


### [251] [TacCoRL: Integrating Tactile Feedback into VLA via Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11743)
*Siyu Ma,Yuqi Liang,Chang Yu,Yunuo Chen,Hao Su,Yixin Zhu,Yin Yang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacCoRL是一种将触觉反馈注入视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的可扩展框架，通过仿真-现实协同训练和基于仿真的强化学习提升接触密集型机器人操作任务的性能，无需大规模触觉预训练或大量真实世界探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉观察往往无法捕捉接触状态，导致在接触密集型任务中表现受限，而直接在真实硬件上收集接触数据成本高且风险大。

Method: 引入触觉输入并学习其对动作响应的调节作用；使用与真实环境对齐的仿真器进行闭环训练；混合仿真与真实轨迹用于热启动触觉条件动作；结合强化学习优化策略，并用监督目标保持与真实数据分布一致。

Result: 在四个双手接触密集型任务上，所提方法平均成功率达72.5%，显著高于基线的50.0%。

Conclusion: TacCoRL能有效融合触觉反馈与VLA模型，通过仿真辅助训练提升真实场景下的策略性能，实现无需在线强化学习或特权信息的直接部署。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide strong visual, language, and action priors for robot manipulation, but visual observations alone often miss the local contact state required for contact-rich tasks. We present TacCoRL, a scalable framework that injects Tactile feedback into VLA policies and improves them through sim-real Co-training and simulation-based reinforcement learning (RL), without requiring large-scale tactile pretraining or extensive real-world contact exploration. The key idea is not only adding touch as an input, but learning how contact readings should modulate action responses in near-failure states that are rare in demonstrations and risky to collect on hardware. We use a real-aligned simulator as a closed-loop training environment for contact interaction. Mixed simulated and real trajectories first warm-start tactile-conditioned actions in the pretrained policy. Reinforcement learning with verifiable task rewards then optimizes the policy using simulated contact rollouts. It reinforces tactile-conditioned actions that lead to task completion, while a supervised objective on real trajectories keeps the refined policy anchored to deployment visual, tactile, and action distributions. The resulting policy transfers directly to the real robot without privileged simulation state or online real-world RL. Across four bimanual contact-rich tasks, the final visuo-tactile policy achieves an average success rate of 72.5%, compared to baseline of 50.0%. Result videos and more details are available at https://tac-corl.github.io/

</details>


### [252] [Blind Dexterous Grasping via Real2Sim2Real Tactile Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11767)
*Shengcheng Luo,Xiyan Huang,Zhe Xu,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于纯触觉盲抓的框架，结合Real2Sim触觉校准、布局感知编码器和扩散策略，在无视觉输入情况下实现了真实机械手上的有效盲抓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决纯触觉盲抓中仿真到现实的触觉鸿沟以及稀疏触觉信号表达能力不足的问题。

Method: 1. 提出Real2Sim触觉校准流程构建可复现真实触觉信号的数字孪生仿真器；2. 使用布局感知的触觉编码器并通过自监督预训练引入传感器几何先验；3. 在仿真中训练物体特定的强化学习专家，并将其成功抓取轨迹聚合为触觉条件下的扩散策略。

Result: 在配备分布式触觉传感的LEAP手上测试了10个已见和10个未见物体，真实世界抓取成功率达27%，无需真实抓取示范或视觉输入。仿真消融实验显示布局感知预训练提升性能，硬件验证表明Real2Sim校准提高了仿真与实际触觉接触的一致性。

Conclusion: 接触事件校准、几何感知的触觉表征学习与基于扩散的策略聚合是实现真实灵巧手纯触觉盲抓的有效路径。

Abstract: Blind grasping with a dexterous hand is a crucial manipulation capability. Nevertheless, learning such tactile-only policies for real robots remains challenging due to the tactile sim-to-real gap and the limited expressiveness of sparse tactile signals. To bridge this gap, we propose a framework for tactile-only blind grasping that is deployable on a physical multi-fingered robotic hand. Our approach combines three key components. First, we introduce a Real2Sim tactile calibration pipeline that constructs a contact-calibrated digital-twin simulator capable of reproducing real tactile signals. Second, we improve the expressiveness of sparse tactile observations using a layout-aware tactile encoder, which incorporates sensor-geometry priors through self-supervised pretraining. Third, to improve generalization to unseen objects, we train object-specific reinforcement-learning experts in the calibrated simulator and aggregate their successful grasp trajectories into a tactile-conditioned Diffusion Policy. We evaluate our method on a physical LEAP Hand equipped with distributed tactile sensing across 10 seen and 10 unseen objects. The deployed policy achieves a 27\% real-world grasp success rate across all 20 objects, without real-world grasping demonstrations or visual input. Simulation ablations show that layout-aware tactile pretraining improves grasping performance, while sensing-level evaluations confirm that Real2Sim calibration increases the consistency of tactile contact events between simulation and hardware. Together, these results suggest that contact-event calibration, geometry-aware tactile representation learning, and diffusion-based policy aggregation provide an effective path toward tactile-only blind grasping on real dexterous robotic hands. Project page:Dex-Blind-Grasp.github.io.

</details>


### [253] [Human-Guided Co-Manipulation of Carbon Fiber Plies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11818)
*Rami Ojanen,James Fant-Male,Roel Pieters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在碳纤维布层共操作中不同控制方法的优缺点，并提出结合语音指令、基于视觉的手腕跟踪以及力与柔顺控制的多模态方法作为最优解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于柔性材料的可变形性，其自动化处理具有挑战性，而完全手动操作又效率低下且不符合人体工程学，因此需要一种能够兼顾人类参与和效率提升的人机协作方案。

Method: 研究对比了多种用于碳纤维布层共操作的控制方法，并提出了融合语音命令、视觉手腕追踪和力控与柔顺控制的多模态控制策略。

Result: 在受控环境中评估了各种控制方法的优缺点，发现多模态组合方法在实现完整且直观的任务控制方面表现最佳。

Conclusion: 结合语音、视觉跟踪和力控的多模态控制方法是实现柔性材料人机共操作的最有效方式。

Abstract: The handling of flexible materials is a difficult task to fully automate due to the challenges caused by the deformability of these types of objects. Meanwhile, a fully manual process can be ergonomically challenging, tedious and inefficient. Thus, human-robot collaboration (HRC) and cooperative manipulation (co-manipulation) have received increasing interest in this field as they enable human involvement when needed while also improving productivity. To enable efficient co-manipulation and interaction between the human operator and the robot, different modalities and control methods are required. In this paper, we present and examine different control methods for co-manipulation of carbon fiber plies, evaluating the pros and cons of each method in a controlled setting. We propose that a multimodal combination of speech commands, wrist-tracking through vision, and force with compliant control would provide the best solution for complete and intuitive control of the task.

</details>


### [254] [Modular Anthropomorphic Hand Design via Multi-Parameter Finger Benchmarking and Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11826)
*Yu Zhang,Huijiang Wang,Josie Hughes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过模块化优化手指设计来提升灵巧机器人手整体性能的框架，并通过定量基准和任务验证展示了其在多物体抓取和拧灯泡等任务中的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人手设计优化方法通常缺乏系统性，且只考虑单一性能指标，难以实现全面比较和针对性改进。因此需要一种能够综合评估手指设计对整体手性能影响的新方法。

Method: 开发了一个可模块化集成手指的全遥控机器人手平台，通过机制导向和任务相关的多种定量指标评估不同手指原型（包括关节、骨骼、皮肤和传感器布局的变化），并在整合到完整手上之前进行快速筛选。

Result: 该框架成功应用于开发具有优化手指的仿人机器人手，在多物体抓取、拧灯泡等任务中表现出显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 优化单个手指的设计可以显著提高整个机器人手的灵巧性和任务表现，模块化方法为系统化设计和评估提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Designing anthropomorphic dexterous robotic hands remains challenging as the design space straddles morphology, actuation, and sensing properties, and performance metrics span both task-dependent and task-agnostic. Existing optimization methods are often unstructured or consider only a single performance metric, limiting systematic comparison and targeted refinement. While the design considerations of the entire hand are significant, the individual finger properties play a key role in dexterity. By developing a robotic hand platform where fingers can be modularly integrated into a full teleoperated hand, we propose that optimizing the fingers can significantly improve overall hand performance. This approach enables rapid screening of different finger-level prototypes through a number of quantitative benchmarks before their integration into the hand for task-level validation. Candidate finger designs (incorporating variations in joint, bone, skin, and sensor placement) are assessed using both mechanism-oriented and task-relevant metrics, which establish a quantitative link between component design and full hand embodiment. The framework is validated through the development of an anthropomorphic robotic hand with optimized fingers, demonstrating how these fingers enable performance improvements across tasks, including multi-object grasping and light bulb screwing.

</details>


### [255] [Critic Architecture Matters: Dual vs. Unified Critics for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11891)
*Mehmet Turan Yardımcı*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在人形机器人多目标强化学习中，使用双批评家（dual-critic）架构相比单一批评家（unified-critic）显著提升了运动与操作任务的协调效率，且效果优于奖励工程优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在多目标强化学习中如何有效协调人形机器人的运动与操作行为，特别是批评家架构对策略学习的影响。

Method: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上，通过NVIDIA Isaac Lab中的13级顺序课程训练多目标策略，对比统一批评家与双批评家架构在标准化评估中的表现。

Result: 双批评家策略比统一批评家策略目标到达速度快3.5倍，吞吐量高2倍，验证到达率更高（65.2% vs. 53.8%），而额外的防游戏化奖励机制未带来进一步提升。

Conclusion: 批评家架构是多目标人形机器人强化学习中的关键设计选择，其影响超过奖励工程，尤其在微调模仿学习策略时，统一批评家可能因梯度竞争抑制已有行为。

Abstract: Multi-objective reinforcement learning for humanoid robots must coordinate locomotion and manipulation within a single policy. A natural design choice is whether to use a single (unified) critic that estimates the combined value of all objectives, or separate (dual) critics with disjoint reward signals. We present a controlled comparison on the Unitree G1 humanoid (23 active DoF) in NVIDIA Isaac Lab, training loco-manipulation policies through a sequential curriculum spanning 13 levels from stationary reaching to walking with variable-orientation targets. In standardized evaluation, dual-critic policies reach targets 3.5$\times$ faster (6.5 vs. 22.6 simulation steps), achieve 2$\times$ higher throughput (14.3 vs. 7.0 validated reaches per 1,000 steps), and attain higher validated reach rates (65.2% vs. 53.8%) compared to the unified-critic policy. Notably, additional anti-gaming reward mechanisms provide no further improvement beyond the architectural change alone (60.9% vs. 65.2%). These results have direct implications for the emerging paradigm of RL fine-tuning of imitation-learned policies: when refining a pre-trained manipulation policy with RL, a unified critic risks suppressing the learned behavior through competing locomotion gradients. These findings demonstrate that critic architecture is a primary - and often overlooked - design choice in multi-objective humanoid RL, with greater impact than reward engineering on reaching efficiency.

</details>


### [256] [DuoBench: A Reproducible Benchmark for Bimanual Manipulation in Simulation and the Real World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11901)
*Tobias Jülg,Seongjin Bien,Simon Hilber,Yannik Blei,Pierre Krack,Maximilian Li,Sven Parusel,Rudolf Lioutikov,Florian Walter,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DuoBench是一个面向FR3 Duo平台的双臂操作基准框架，包含11个任务和细粒度评估方案，支持仿真与真实环境中的策略评测，并提供人类遥操作数据集以促进双臂模仿学习与视觉-语言-动作策略的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准未能充分捕捉双臂机器人协调过程中的复杂性与失败模式，缺乏对双臂操作中控制挑战的系统性评估工具。

Method: 提出DuoBench框架，涵盖四个协调类别的11项任务，采用基于阶段的评估机制实现细粒度语义失败分析，并提供可复现的任务配置与3D打印组件，在仿真和真实环境中部署并收集人类遥操作数据集。

Result: 在仿真和真实硬件上评估了多种双臂模仿学习与视觉-语言-动作策略，发现当前方法在早期交互、双臂并行执行及仿真到现实迁移方面仍存在显著挑战。

Conclusion: DuoBench为双臂操作策略提供了可复现的测试平台，有助于诊断现有方法的失败模式，并推动未来双臂策略学习的发展。

Abstract: Bimanual robot systems substantially expand manipulation capabilities, but coordinating two arms introduces additional control complexity and failure modes that are not well captured by existing benchmarks. We introduce DuoBench, an extensible benchmarking framework for bimanual manipulation policies on the FR3 Duo platform. DuoBench comprises eleven tasks spanning four coordination categories, implemented in simulation and partially reproduced in the real world through reproducible task recipes with 3D-printable assets. In addition, we propose a stage-based evaluation scheme that supports fine-grained semantic failure analysis beyond binary success and provide human-teleoperated datasets for all benchmark tasks. We benchmark several dual-arm imitation-learning and vision-language-action policies in simulation and on real hardware. Our results show that current policies remain challenged by bimanual manipulation, particularly in early interaction stages, parallel arm execution, and transfer between simulation and real-world settings. DuoBench provides a reproducible testbed for diagnosing these failure modes and studying future methods for dual-arm policy learning. Code, datasets, and videos are available at https://duobench.github.io/

</details>


### [257] [Deformable In-Hand Slip-Aware Tactile Sensor with Integrated Velocity, Force/Torque, and Pressure Map Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11952)
*Gabriel Arslan Waltersson,Yiannis Karayiannidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种用于手内操作的新型触觉传感器，集成了速度、力/扭矩和压力图感知功能，具有可变形接触垫，适用于多种表面和材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现更精确的手内操作控制，需要一种能够同时感知多种物理量的触觉传感器。

Method: 设计并制造了一种集成多种传感模态的柔性结构传感器，采用标准PCB制造与快速成型技术结合的方法。

Result: 该传感器能够在广泛的材料上稳定跟踪平面和曲面，并通过一系列实验验证了其性能和局限性。

Conclusion: 这是首个在单一柔性结构中整合速度、力/扭矩和压力图感知的传感器，具备低成本和快速制造的优势。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel tactile sensor for in-hand manipulation with slip-aware control that integrates velocity, force/torque, and pressure map sensing into a single device with a deformable contact pad. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first sensor to combine these sensing modalities within a single compliant structure. The sensor features a deformable contact surface and can robustly track both flat and curved surfaces across a wide range of materials. Its performance is evaluated through a comprehensive set of experiments that highlight both its capabilities and limitations. The sensor is designed for rapid and low-cost fabrication using a combination of standard PCB manufacturing and rapid prototyping techniques.

</details>


### [258] [MPPI-based Informative Trajectory Planning for Search and Capture of Drifting Targets with ASVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12019)
*Sanjeev Ramkumar Sudha,Marija Popović,Erlend M. Coates*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于多漂移目标搜索与捕获的混合规划框架，结合MPPI控制与纯追踪策略，在动态环境中实现高效搜索与安全捕获，并通过仿真与实船试验验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态海洋环境中，传统的目标跟踪方法多依赖简单引导策略和短期预测，难以有效平衡搜索未知区域与持续跟踪已知漂移目标的需求，因此需要一种能够长期规划并兼顾探索与追踪的智能决策方法。

Method: 采用基于模型预测路径积分（MPPI）的时空信息规划方法，作为采样型模型预测控制，直接优化长时域的连续轨迹以生成底层运动指令；通过多目标代价函数平衡搜索与跟踪任务，并确保轨迹的安全性与可行性；在拦截阶段切换至纯追踪控制器以实现对移动目标的物理捕获。

Result: 实验表明，所提规划器在性能上优于选定的基线方法，能够在复杂动态环境中有效搜索并跟踪多个漂移目标；并通过自主水面艇（ASV）的实地试验验证了该方法的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 该混合规划框架能有效解决动态环境中多漂移目标的搜索与捕获问题，通过结合MPPI长时域优化与纯追踪精确控制，实现了探索与跟踪的平衡，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Autonomous surface vehicles offer an efficient solution for environmental cleanup as well as search and rescue operations in open waters. Targets in these settings drift continuously, so efficient search must balance exploration of unobserved regions with tracking of known targets. However, most target tracking and pursuit scenarios consider simple guidance behaviours and short-term predictions for decision-making. In this letter, we address the problem of search and capture of multiple drifting targets, such as litter, in dynamic environments, using a hybrid planning framework. A key aspect of our strategy is a spatiotemporal informative planning method based on model predictive path integral (MPPI) control, a sampling-based model predictive control approach. The planner directly generates kinematic-level commands by optimising continuous trajectories over long horizons. A multi-objective cost balances search and tracking objectives while ensuring safe, feasible trajectories. In the interception stage, we switch to a pure pursuit guidance controller for the physical capture of moving targets. Experiments show that our planner outperforms the chosen planning baselines. Finally, we validate our approach in field trials with an ASV.

</details>


### [259] [Learning Unions of Convex Sets via Invertible Latent Decomposition for Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12027)
*Taerim Yoon,Dongho Kang,Kisang Park,Junha Cha,Stelian Coros,Sungjoon Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ILD（可逆潜在分解）的框架，用于在复杂环境中进行无碰撞路径规划。该方法结合了显式和隐式表示的优点，通过学习可逆映射和潜在空间中的凸多面体联合来实现高效且可靠的路径规划，并引入可见性引导采样（VGS）以保持凸集连通性，在多种环境中表现出更高的规划成功率和实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无碰撞路径规划方法中，显式表示难以扩展到高维配置空间，而隐式表示缺乏严格的无碰撞保证。因此需要一种既能保持可扩展性又能提供严格安全性保障的新方法。

Method: 提出ILD框架，联合学习一个可逆映射和潜在空间中的一组显式凸多面体；利用该可逆映射将路径从潜在空间解码回原始配置空间，同时保持可行性；并设计可见性引导采样（VGS）策略以增强凸集之间的连通性。

Result: 在2D导航、6自由度和14自由度操作环境中，ILD相比先前方法实现了更广的覆盖范围、更好的集合间连通性和更高的路径规划成功率，测试时精炼后无任何误报；在14自由度双手机器人上实现了实时无碰撞规划，并能在真实场景中适应几何变化。

Conclusion: ILD有效弥合了显式与隐式碰撞空间表示之间的差距，能够在复杂高维空间中实现安全、高效且可扩展的路径规划，具备实际部署能力。

Abstract: Collision-free path planning in cluttered, real-world environments relies on a representation of the collision-free space, and existing representations broadly fall into two
  categories. Explicit representations, such as unions of convex sets, can be plugged into optimization-based planners as hard collision-free constraints, but their parameters scale
  poorly with configuration-space dimension. Implicit representations, by contrast, are flexible and scale well to complex geometries, yet typically lack such guarantees. We bridge this
  gap with ILD (Invertible Latent Decomposition), a framework that jointly learns an invertible mapping and a union of explicit convex polytopes in the resulting latent space. Planning
  is carried out over these latent convex sets, and the invertible mapping decodes the resulting paths back to the original configuration space while preserving feasibility with
  respect to the refined explicit safe regions. We further propose Visibility-Guided Sampling (VGS) to keep the convex sets connected for path planning. Across 2D navigation, 6-DoF, and
  14-DoF manipulation environments, ILD achieves broader coverage, better inter-set connectivity, and higher path-planning success rates than prior baselines, with zero observed false
  positives after test-time refinement. On a 14-DoF bimanual manipulator, we further demonstrate real-time collision-free planning, with test-time refinement adapting to scene-geometry
  changes during real-world deployment on a single 6-DoF arm.

</details>


### [260] [VICX: Generalizable Robot Manipulation via Video Generation and In-Context Operator Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12028)
*Song Chen,Linyan Xiang,Ying Zhou,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VICX是一种解耦的闭环操作框架，通过视频生成模型和任务无关的视觉到状态映射网络（V2T-ICON），实现跨任务和跨具身的泛化操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决机器人操作中高层视觉规划与具体执行之间的鸿沟，提升在未见场景下的任务级推理与执行泛化能力。

Method: 采用冻结的视频生成模型生成视觉语言条件的高层视觉计划，利用基于分割提取的臂部帧观测和检索的图像-状态对作为上下文提示，由V2T-ICON网络将视觉计划映射为可执行的状态轨迹。

Result: 在Meta-World实验中，VICX实现了跨任务泛化、闭环自纠错和跨具身迁移能力。

Conclusion: VICX实现了任务语义与机器人执行的双重泛化，为通用机器人操作提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Generalizable robot manipulation requires not only task-level reasoning over unseen scenes, but also reliable grounding of visual plans into embodiment-specific execution. To bridge this gap, we propose VICX (Video generation and In-Context eXecution), a decoupled closed-loop manipulation framework. In VICX, a frozen video generation model produces vision-language-conditioned high-level visual plans, while a Video-to-Trajectory In-Context Operator Network (V2T-ICON) serves as the task-agnostic interface that grounds these plans into executable robot-state trajectories. To improve execution generalization, V2T-ICON operates on segmentation-extracted arm-only frame observations and uses retrieved image-state pairs as in-context prompts, allowing a robust and generalizable visual-to-state mapping at inference time without parameter updates. Experiments on Meta-World show that VICX supports cross-task generalization, closed-loop self-correction, and cross-embodiment transfer, demonstrating dual generalization across both task semantics and robot execution. The project webpage can be found here: https://scaling-group.github.io/vicx/.

</details>


### [261] [KinematicRL: A Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning Framework For Social Navigation With Kinodynamic Feasibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12042)
*Zhiming Xu,Haodong Yang,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen,Chenpeng Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于社会导航的深度强化学习框架KinematicRL，通过引入高阶控制、仅使用2D LiDAR的聚类人体跟踪方法以及无偏残差门控模块，有效缩小了仿真到现实的差距，并实现了在真实差速驱动机器人上的部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度强化学习在社会导航中的应用受限于简化的动力学模型和依赖特定传感器的人体状态估计，导致仿真到现实迁移困难。本文旨在构建一个统一框架以提升策略在真实环境中的可行性和适应性。

Method: 1) 采用二阶控制输入作为动作空间，并通过随机迭代LQR预训练策略；2) 设计仅基于2D LiDAR的聚类人体跟踪流程，结合空间和速度相似性进行行人关联；3) 引入无偏残差门控结构以平衡反应式与记忆式行为，适应动态人群规模。
